namespace = POD_chargen

scripted_trigger POD_is_landed_or_valid_for_landless = {
	trigger_if = {
		limit = { trait:$TRAIT$ = { has_trait_flag = landless_unavailable } }
		is_landed = yes
	}
}

scripted_effect POD_setup_faith_selection_event = {
	set_variable = {
		name  = chargen_religion
		value = $RELIGION$
	}
	custom_tooltip = POD_chargen_faith_selection
}

scripted_effect POD_trigger_faith_selection_event = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = chargen_religion }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name  = next_event
			value = event_id:$NEXT_EVENT$
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable_list = chargen_faiths }
			clear_variable_list = chargen_faiths
		}
		var:chargen_religion = {
			ordered_faith = {
				order_by = num_county_followers
				min = 0
				max = 100
				check_range_bounds = no
				root = {
					add_to_variable_list = {
						name   = chargen_faiths
						target = prev
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_in_list = {
			variable = chargen_faiths
			position = 0
			prev = {
				set_variable = {
					name  = chargen_faith_selection
					value = prev
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.6000
	}
	else = {
		trigger_event = $NEXT_EVENT$
	}
}

scripted_effect POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = {
	add_to_variable_list = {
		name   = chargen_faiths
		target = $FAITH$
	}
}

scripted_effect POD_trigger_faith_selection_event_with_preexisting_faith_list = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = chargen_religion
			has_variable_list = chargen_faiths
		}
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name  = next_event
			value = event_id:$NEXT_EVENT$
		}
		ordered_in_list = {
			variable = chargen_faiths
			position = 0
			prev = {
				set_variable = {
					name  = chargen_faith_selection
					value = prev
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.6000
	}
	else = {
		trigger_event = $NEXT_EVENT$
	}
}

scripted_effect POD_setup_vampire_house_head_event = {
	$HOUSE_HEAD$ = {
		save_scope_as = house_head
	}
	set_house = scope:house_head.house
	trigger_event = POD_chargen.510
}


POD_chargen.1 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1.desc

	theme = caitiff
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
	
	
	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_in_search_of_iniquity"
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1.option.a
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1.option.a.tt
		add_character_flag = POD_used_character_generator
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_faith = faith:viahumanitatis
			
			set_local_variable = {
				name = saved_immortal_age
				value = POD_visual_age
			}
			
			POD_remove_all_supernatural_atribute_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_initialization

			save_scope_as = character_being_changed
			
			if = {
				limit = { is_from_ruler_designer = yes }
				house = { save_scope_as = coa_target }
			}
			else = {
				POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
				create_character = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = created_vampirelord
					location = scope:character_being_changed.capital_province
					age = 1200
					culture = scope:character_being_changed.culture
					faith = scope:character_being_changed.faith
					dynasty = generate
					gender = male
					random_traits = yes
				}
				
				set_house = scope:created_vampirelord.house
				
				scope:created_vampirelord = {
					death = {
						death_reason = death_mysterious
					}
				}
				POD_remove_parent_relation_effect = yes
			}
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { is_from_ruler_designer = no }
			trigger_event = {
				id = podintroductions.6000
				days = 3
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1.option.b
		
		POD_after_intro_initiation_effect = yes
	}
	
}

POD_chargen.2 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.2.t
	desc = {
		desc = POD_chargen.2.desc
		triggered_desc = {
			trigger = { is_landed = no }
			desc = {
				desc = double_line_break
				desc = POD_chargen.2.desc.landless
			}
		}
	}

	theme = caitiff
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
	
	immediate = {
		save_scope_as = house_head
		add_character_flag = had_POD_character_initialization

		play_music_cue = "mood_in_search_of_iniquity"
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2.option.a
		
		trigger = {
			POD_is_expod_enabled = yes
			OR = {
				POD_is_landed_or_valid_for_landless = { TRAIT = terrestrial_exalted }
			}
		}
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }
		
		custom_tooltip = chargen_fera_tt
	
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.4000 }
	}

	option = {
		name = trait_vampire
		
		trigger = { POD_is_landed_or_valid_for_landless = { TRAIT = vampire } }
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }

		add_trait = vampire

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = local_var:saved_immortal_age }
				set_immortal_age = local_var:saved_immortal_age
			}
		}
	
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.3 }
	}

	option = {
		name = trait_kueijin
		
		trigger = { POD_is_landed_or_valid_for_landless = { TRAIT = kueijin } }
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }

		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }

		add_trait = revenant
	
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1300 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2.option.b
		
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				POD_is_landed_or_valid_for_landless = { TRAIT = werewolf }
				POD_is_landed_or_valid_for_landless = { TRAIT = bastet }

			}
		}
		show_as_unavailable = { always = yes }

	# option = {

POD_chargen.35 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.34.t
	desc = POD_chargen.34.desc

	theme = gargoyle_coa

	left_portrait = {
		character = character:POD_Rock_Lord_01 #Rock Lord
		animation = idle
	}

	# Independent
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.35.option.a
		add_trait = vampgeneration11
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}
	}
	
	# Rocklord Sire
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.35.option.b
		character:POD_Rock_Lord_01 = {
			save_scope_as = house_head
		}
		
		set_father = scope:house_head
		set_house = scope:house_head.house
		add_trait = vampgeneration10
		
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}
	}
}

POD_chargen.510 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.510.t
	desc = POD_chargen.510.desc

	theme = family
	left_portrait = root
	right_portrait = scope:house_head
	
	immediate = {
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = house_head
			}
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}
			house = {
				save_scope_as = house
			}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.a
		
		house.house_head = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
		}
			
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampirechilde
		}
		root = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
		}
	
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = no
				}
			}
			set_father = scope:house_head
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = yes
					}
			}
			set_mother = scope:house_head
		}

		scope:created_vampirechilde = {
			POD_character_vampire_inherit_generation_effect = yes
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.b
		add_trait = vampgeneration10

		# POD_add_house_heritage_modifier_effect = { HOUSE_HEAD = scope:house_head }

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.c
		add_trait = vampgeneration7
		
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes
		POD_add_house_heritage_modifier_effect = { HOUSE_HEAD = scope:house_head }

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}
	}
	
}

# Clan effects
POD_chargen.1000 = {
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		# Clan effects
		if = {
			limit = { # Prevent Tzimisce face randomization for custom characters
				OR = {
					is_tzimisce_trigger = yes
					# only the bloodlines with vicissitude
					is_chibo_trigger = yes
					is_monomi_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			add_character_flag = had_POD_character_tzimisce_looks
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { # Allow the player to choose whether their custom Nosferatu gets further nosferization
				is_nosferatu_trigger = yes
				is_mnemachien_trigger = no
			}
			add_character_flag = had_POD_character_nosferatu_looks
			trigger_event = { id = POD_maintenance.28 days = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			POD_character_vampire_clan_special_effect = yes
		}
		
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.1009 #Predator types
	}
}

POD_chargen.1009 = {
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
		has_predator_type_trigger = no
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = ventrue }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.1100 #Ventrue Feeding Preferences
		}
		else_if = {
			# Mnemachien clan curse forces consensualist feeding type
			limit = { is_mnemachien_trigger = yes }
			add_secret = { type = secret_predatortype_consensualist }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = masquerade0_modifier }
				POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.1010 #Predator types
		}
	}
}

POD_chargen.1010 = { #Predator types
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1010.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1010.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = { #Farmer
		name = POD_chargen.1010.option.a
		trigger = {
			is_ventrue_trigger = no
			NAND = {
				is_kishu_trigger = yes
				POD_suppress_clan_curse_trigger = no
			}
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency0_modifier
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency1_modifier
				AND = {
					has_animalism_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency2_modifier
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency3_modifier
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency4_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency4_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency5_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency6_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency7_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency8_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency9_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency10_modifier }
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_farmer
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_piety = major_piety_gain
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_hunter }
				}
				add_trait = lifestyle_hunter
			}
			add_animal_lifestyle_xp = minor_lifestyle_xp
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_animalism_trigger = yes
					has_protean_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = protean
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_animalism_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = animalism
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1180 }
		# Ends CharGen
	}

	option = { #Human Predator Types
		name = POD_chargen.1010.option.b
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1020 }
	}
	
	option = { #Bloodleech
		name = POD_chargen.1010.option.c
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_bloodleech
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_prowess_skill = 1
			POD_bloodpotency_increase_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_celerity_trigger = yes
					has_protean_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = protean
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_celerity_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = celerity
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1180 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.1030 = { #Charm

	left_portrait = root

	}

	option = { #Siren
		name = POD_chargen.1030.option.a
		trigger = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { is_nosferatu_trigger = yes }

			}
			trigger_else_if = {

				limit = {
					NOT  = { has_trait = beauty_good }
				}

			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_presence_trigger = yes
					has_fortitude_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = fortitude
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_presence_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = presence
			}
			if = {

POD_chargen.1040 = { #Deceit
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1040.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1040.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = { #Cauchemar
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.a
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_cauchemar
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_intrigue_skill = 1
			add_gold = minor_gold_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
					has_auspex_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = auspex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = obfuscate
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
				}
				add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_auspex_trigger = yes
				}
				add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1180 }
	}

	option = { #Familias
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.b
		trigger = {
			is_ventrue_trigger = no
			POD_splat_can_herd_trigger = yes
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_familias
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			#add_diplomacy_skill = 1
			#Spawn herd family
			hidden_effect = {
				POD_create_herd_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:herd }
					if = {
						limit = { scope:herd = { is_male = yes } }
						scope:herd = { save_scope_as = father }
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_young_woman_character
							save_scope_as = mother
						}
						POD_herdify_effect = { TARGET = scope:mother }
					}
					else = {
						scope:herd = { save_scope_as = mother }
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_young_man_character
							save_scope_as = father
						}
						POD_herdify_effect = { TARGET = scope:father }
					}
					scope:father = { marry = scope:mother }
					while = {
						count = 2
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_child_character
							save_scope_as = child
						}
						scope:child = {
							set_father = scope:father
							set_mother = scope:mother
						}
						POD_herdify_effect = { TARGET = scope:child }
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1040.option.b.herd
			#Add herd hook to generated family
			custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1040.option.b.tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = masquerade0_modifier
				}
				POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
			}
			add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
					has_animalism_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = animalism
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = dominate
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
					}
				add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1180 }
	}
	
	option = { #Footpad
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.c
		trigger = {
			is_ventrue_trigger = no
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_footpad
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_intrigue_skill = 1
			add_intrigue_lifestyle_xp = 200
			add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
					has_potence_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = potence
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
				}
				add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_potence_trigger = yes
				}
				add_choleric_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = obfuscate
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1180 }
	}
	
	option = { #Non-Human Predator Types
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.d
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1020 }
	}
}

# Ghoul intro
POD_chargen.1200 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1200.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1200.desc
	theme = ghoul
	
	# Bound Ghoul
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1200.a
		
		trigger = { is_unbondable_trigger = no }
		
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1200.a.tt
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1200.a.tt2
		
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = charjoin_become_ghoul_flag
			days = 7
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_has_illegal_government_trigger = yes }
				POD_adopt_correct_government_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		# fallback in case the player didn't pick anyone
		trigger_event = {
			id = POD_ghoul_maintenance.3
			days = 8
		}
	}
	
	# Masterless Ghoul
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1200.b
		
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1200.b.tt
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1200.b.tt2
		
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOR = {
							religion = { is_in_family = rf_kindred }
							POD_has_valid_splat_agnostic_faith = yes
						}
						# Character generator sets you to Via Humanitatis by default
						# make sure you still get to choose your faith in that case
						AND = {
							has_character_flag = POD_used_character_generator
							has_faith = faith:viahumanitatis
						}
					}
				}
				# set a vampire religion beforehand so the masquerade modifier gets initialized properly in POD_become_ghoul_effect
				POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes
				trigger_event = POD_chargen.710
			}
		
			POD_become_ghoul_effect = yes
			
			# If you managed to roll no disciplines (unlucky!), get Potence as a consolation prize
			if = {
				limit = { has_POD_vampire_discipline_trigger = no }
				add_trait = potence
			}
		
			if = {
				limit = { POD_has_illegal_government_trigger = yes }
				POD_adopt_correct_government_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		trigger_event = {
			id = POD_ghoul_maintenance.2
			days = 1
		}
	}
	
	# Calomena's Forsaken
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1200.c
		
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1200.c.tt
		
		add_trait = supehunter
		
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_become_ghoul_effect = yes
			
			# If you managed to roll no disciplines (unlucky!), get Potence as a consolation prize
			if = {
				limit = { has_POD_vampire_discipline_trigger = no }
				add_trait = potence
			}
		
			if = {
				limit = { POD_has_illegal_government_trigger = yes }
				POD_adopt_correct_government_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		set_character_faith = faith:calomenasforsaken
		
		trigger_event = {
			id = POD_ghoul_maintenance.2
			days = 1
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1200.d
		
		remove_trait = ghoul
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}	
}

POD_chargen.1360 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1360.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1360.desc

	theme = family
	left_portrait = root
	right_portrait = scope:house_head
	
	immediate = {
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = house_head
			}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.a
		trigger = {
			age < scope:house_head.age
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = no
				}
			}
			set_father = scope:house_head
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = yes
					}
			}
			set_mother = scope:house_head
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.b
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.c
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes

		#Add house heritage modifier check here

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370}
		}
	}

POD_chargen.1400 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1400.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1400.desc

	theme = inquisition
	left_portrait = root
	
	immediate = {
	

		character:791707 = {
			save_scope_as = gauthier
		}
		character:791708 = {
			save_scope_as = leopold
		}
		character:791713 = {
			save_scope_as = maria
		}
		character:791715 = {
			save_scope_as = vittoria
		}
		character:791723 = {
			save_scope_as = aignen
		}
		character:791725 = {
			save_scope_as = rodrigue
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.a

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		set_house = scope:gauthier.house

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.b
		
		set_house = scope:rodrigue.house

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.c
		
		trigger = { is_female = yes }
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:maria.house
		
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.d
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:aignen.house
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.e

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:vittoria.house
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.f
		
		set_house = scope:leopold.house

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
	}

	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.h

	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.i

		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}
	
	after = {
		POD_reset_grand_cities_on_splat_change = yes
	}
}

POD_chargen.1410 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1410.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1410.desc

	theme = inquisition
	left_portrait = root

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.a
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = order_member }
			remove_trait = order_member
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = devoted
			}
		remove_trait = devoted
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_lay_order_modifier }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.b
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_mendicant_order_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.c
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_knightly_order_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.d
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		if = {
			limit = {
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = {
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_contemplative_order_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.e
		
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:character_being_changed
		}
		
		remove_trait = supehunter
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.2632 = {

		name = trait_noonday_sun

		add_trait = noonday_sun
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_setting_sun
		trigger = { OR = { POD_mokole_is_mokole_mbembe_trigger = yes POD_mokole_is_gumagan_trigger = yes } }

	option = {
		name = trait_shrouded_sun

	option = {

	#Makara

		add_trait = vasanta
	}
	option = {

#Suchid Form
POD_chargen.2633 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.2633.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2633.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root
	
	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_variable = suchid_form }
		has_trait = mokole
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2634
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2633.option.a
		trigger = { POD_mokole_is_mokole_mbembe_trigger = yes }
		show_as_tooltip = { add_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_champsa_modifier }
		set_variable = { name = suchid_form value = flag:champsa }
	}
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2633.option.b
		trigger = { POD_mokole_is_mokole_mbembe_trigger = no }
		show_as_tooltip = { add_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_karna_modifier }
		set_variable = { name = suchid_form value = flag:karna }
	}
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2633.option.c
		trigger = { OR = { POD_mokole_is_gumagan_trigger = yes POD_mokole_is_zhong_lung_trigger = yes } }
		show_as_tooltip = { add_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_ora_modifier }
		set_variable = { name = suchid_form value = flag:ora }
	}
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2633.option.d
		trigger = { POD_mokole_is_makara_trigger = yes }
		show_as_tooltip = { add_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_gharial_modifier }
		set_variable = { name = suchid_form value = flag:gharial }
	}
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2633.option.e
		trigger = { POD_mokole_is_makara_trigger = yes }
		show_as_tooltip = { add_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_makara_modifier }
		set_variable = { name = suchid_form value = flag:makara }
	}
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2633.option.f
		trigger = { POD_mokole_is_makara_trigger = yes }
		show_as_tooltip = { add_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_naja_modifier }
		set_variable = { name = suchid_form value = flag:naja }
	}
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2633.option.g
		trigger = { POD_mokole_is_zhong_lung_trigger = yes }
		show_as_tooltip = { add_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_lung_modifier }
		set_variable = { name = suchid_form value = flag:lung }
	}
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2633.option.h
		trigger = { POD_mokole_is_mokole_mbembe_trigger = yes }
		show_as_tooltip = { add_character_modifier = POD_mokole_form_suchid_suchus_modifier }
		set_variable = { name = suchid_form value = flag:suchus }
	}


	after = { trigger_event = POD_chargen.2634 }
}
#Faith
POD_chargen.2634 = {
	type = character_event
	hidden = yes
	
	immediate = {
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:saurian_religion }
		
		POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = { FAITH = faith:children_sobek }
		POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = { FAITH = faith:warriors_entoban }
		POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = { FAITH = faith:cosmic_order }
		POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = { FAITH = faith:dreaming_sands }
		if = {
			limit = { POD_mokole_is_zhong_lung_trigger = yes }
			POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = { FAITH = faith:dragons_zhong_guo }
		}
		else = { POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = { FAITH = faith:emerald_memory } }
		POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = { FAITH = faith:eternal_breath }
		POD_manually_add_faith_to_selection_event = { FAITH = faith:mnetics }
		
		POD_trigger_faith_selection_event_with_preexisting_faith_list = {
			NEXT_EVENT = POD_chargen.2899
		}
	}
}




#Final Initialization
POD_chargen.2899 = {
	hidden = yes

	immediate = {
		POD_fera_initialization_effect = yes

		#Non pre-defined Mokole need to follow their liege's stream
		if = {
			limit = { POD_is_mokole_trigger = yes }
			every_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = { POD_has_fera_type = { TRAIT = trait:mokole } is_ai = yes }
				POD_assign_mokole_stream_effect = yes
			}
			every_courtier_or_guest = {
				limit = { POD_has_fera_type = { TRAIT = trait:mokole } }
				POD_assign_mokole_stream_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Demon
POD_chargen.2900 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.2900.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2900.desc

	theme = demon
	
	trigger = { NOT = { religion = { is_in_family = rf_demons } } }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		POD_change_government_if_landed = { GOVERNMENT = infernal_court_government }
		POD_demon_initialization_effect = yes
		trigger_event = {
			id = POD_chargen.3000
			days = 7
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = avarice
		
		set_character_faith = faith:avarice
	}
			
	option = {
		name = envy
		
		set_character_faith = faith:envy
	}

	option = {
		name = gluttony
		
		set_character_faith = faith:gluttony
	}
	
	option = {
		name = lust
		
		set_character_faith = faith:lust
	}

	option = {
		name = pride
		
		set_character_faith = faith:pride
	}

	option = {
		name = sloth
		
		set_character_faith = faith:sloth
	}

	option = {
		name = wrath
		
		set_character_faith = faith:wrath
	}
	
	after = {
		POD_change_government_if_landed = { GOVERNMENT = infernal_court_government }
		POD_demon_initialization_effect = yes
		trigger_event = {
			id = POD_chargen.3000
			days = 7
		}
	}
}

POD_chargen.3000 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3000.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3000.desc

	theme = demon
	
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = dynasty }
			dynasty = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_dynasty_modifier = namaru_dynasty_modifier
						has_dynasty_modifier = asharu_dynasty_modifier
						has_dynasty_modifier = annunaki_dynasty_modifier
						has_dynasty_modifier = neberu_dynasty_modifier
						has_dynasty_modifier = lammasu_dynasty_modifier
						has_dynasty_modifier = rabisu_dynasty_modifier
						has_dynasty_modifier = halaku_dynasty_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = { }
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3000.option.a
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3000.option.a.tt
		
		POD_setup_demonic_house_modifier_effect = { HOUSE = asharu }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3000.option.b
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3000.option.b.tt
		
		POD_setup_demonic_house_modifier_effect = { HOUSE = rabisu }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3000.option.c
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3000.option.c.tt
		
		POD_setup_demonic_house_modifier_effect = { HOUSE = halaku }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3000.option.d
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3000.option.d.tt
		
		POD_setup_demonic_house_modifier_effect = { HOUSE = namaru }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3000.option.e
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3000.option.e.tt
		
		POD_setup_demonic_house_modifier_effect = { HOUSE = lammasu }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3000.option.f
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3000.option.f.tt
		
		POD_setup_demonic_house_modifier_effect = { HOUSE = annunaki }
	}
	
	option = {

# Fae
POD_chargen.3100 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3100.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3100.desc

	theme = fae
	
	trigger = { has_kith_trigger = no }

	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_born_in_a_dream"
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = eshu }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3701
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = kithain }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3700
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = jinn }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3700
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = african_kiths }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3701
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = hsien }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3702
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = phyla }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3799
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_kithain
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3200
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_jinn
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3300
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_african_kiths
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3400
	}
	
	# option = {
	# 	name = trait_hsien
	# 	trigger_event = POD_chargen.3500
		
	# 	trigger = { has_trait = changeling }
	# 	show_as_unavailable = { has_trait = fae }
	# }
	
	option = {
		name = trait_phyla
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3600
		
		trigger = { has_trait = fae }
		show_as_unavailable = { has_trait = changeling }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3100.option.f
		trigger = {
			has_trait = fae
			exists = scope:character_being_changed
		}
		
		remove_trait = fae
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3100.option.g
		trigger = {
			has_trait = changeling
			exists = scope:character_being_changed
		}
		
		remove_trait = changeling
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.3700 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3700.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3700.desc

	theme = fae
	
	option = {
		name = winter_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:winter_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = spring_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:spring_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = summer_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:summer_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = autumn_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:autumn_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = solstice_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:solstice_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3700.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				religion = religion:winter_court_religion
				religion = religion:spring_court_religion
				religion = religion:summer_court_religion
				religion = religion:autumn_court_religion
				religion = religion:solstice_court_religion
				AND = {
					has_trait = jinn
					OR = {
						religion = religion:ojo_religion
						religion = religion:iku_religion
					}
				}
				POD_has_valid_splat_agnostic_faith = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		POD_trigger_faith_selection_event = { NEXT_EVENT = POD_chargen.3799 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.3799 = {
	hidden = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = fae }
			POD_fae_initialization_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = changeling }
			POD_changeling_initialization_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = pooka }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3800
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			trigger_event = {
				id = POD_chargen.3900
				days = 10
			}
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3901
		}
	}
}

POD_chargen.3900 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3900.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3900.desc
	theme = medicine
	left_portrait = { 
		character = character:POD_fae_character_77
		animation = idle
	}
	right_portrait = character:POD_fae_character_79	
	
	trigger = {
		has_trait = kithain
	}

	option = { # Rat Route
		name = POD_chargen.3900.a
		every_independent_ruler = {
			limit = { religion = { is_in_family = rf_wyrm } }
			add_trait = oathbreaker
		}
		character:POD_fae_character_72 = { add_trait = oathbreaker }
		character:POD_fae_character_77 = { add_trait = oathbreaker }
		character:POD_fae_character_79 = { add_trait = oathbreaker }
		character:POD_fae_character_81 = { add_trait = oathbreaker }
	}

	option = { # Cat Route
		name = POD_chargen.3900.b
		trigger = { has_trait = pooka }
		custom_tooltip = unlock_convert_to_catlover_scheme
		
		add_character_flag = prevent_black_death_route_cat
	}
}

POD_chargen.3910 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3910.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3910.desc
	theme = fae

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			calc_true_if = {
				amount >= 2
				has_trait = art_chicanery
				has_trait = art_legerdemain
				has_trait = art_primal
				has_trait = art_soothsay
				has_trait = art_wayfare
				has_trait = art_oneiromancy
				has_trait = art_metamorphosis
				has_trait = art_pyretics
				has_trait = art_skycraft
			}
		}
		NOT = { exists = scope:prevent_art }
	}

	option = {
		name = trait_art_chicanery
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_chicanery } }
		add_trait = art_chicanery
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_art_legerdemain
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_legerdemain } }
		add_trait = art_legerdemain
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_art_primal
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_primal } }
		add_trait = art_primal
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_art_soothsay
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_soothsay } }
		add_trait = art_soothsay
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_art_wayfare
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_wayfare } }
		add_trait = art_wayfare
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_art_oneiromancy
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_oneiromancy } }
		add_trait = art_oneiromancy
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_art_metamorphosis
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_metamorphosis } }
		add_trait = art_metamorphosis
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_art_pyretics
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_pyretics } }
		add_trait = art_pyretics
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = trait_art_skycraft
		trigger = { NOT = { has_trait = art_skycraft } }
		add_trait = art_skycraft
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3910.effect
	}
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3910.a
		save_scope_as = prevent_art
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3910
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		clear_saved_scope = prevent_art
	}
}

POD_chargen.4010 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.4010.t
	desc = POD_chargen.4010.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root

	trigger = {
		dynasty = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					THIS = dynasty:POD_exalted_ophris
					THIS = dynasty:POD_exalted_dalca
					THIS = dynasty:POD_exalted_burano
					THIS = dynasty:POD_exalted_barkhad
					THIS = dynasty:POD_exalted_xia
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_ophris
		set_house = house:house_ophris
		show_as_tooltip = { dynasty:POD_exalted_ophris = { add_dynasty_modifier = dynn_ophris_modifier } }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_dalca
		set_house = house:house_dalca
		show_as_tooltip = { dynasty:POD_exalted_dalca = { add_dynasty_modifier = dynn_dalca_modifier } }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_burano
		set_house = house:house_burano
		show_as_tooltip = { dynasty:POD_exalted_burano = { add_dynasty_modifier = dynn_burano_modifier } }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_barkhad
		set_house = house:house_barkhad
		show_as_tooltip = { dynasty:POD_exalted_barkhad = { add_dynasty_modifier = dynn_barkhad_modifier } }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_xia
		set_house = house:house_xia
		show_as_tooltip = { dynasty:POD_exalted_xia = { add_dynasty_modifier = dynn_xia_modifier } }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.4010.a
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.4011
		save_scope_as = ignore_next_event
		# custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.4010.a.tt # Just commented, maybe will be useful in future.
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:ignore_next_event } }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.4012
		}
		clear_saved_scope = ignore_next_event
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.4012
	}
}

POD_chargen.4011 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.4011.t
	desc = POD_chargen.4011.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_ophris
		dynasty ?= { add_dynasty_modifier = heritage_ophris_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_dalca
		dynasty ?= { add_dynasty_modifier = heritage_dalca_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_burano
		dynasty ?= { add_dynasty_modifier = heritage_burano_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_barkhad
		dynasty ?= { add_dynasty_modifier = heritage_barkhad_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = dynn_exalted_xia
		dynasty ?= { add_dynasty_modifier = heritage_xia_modifier }
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.4012
	}
}

POD_chargen.4012 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.4012.t
	desc = POD_chargen.4012.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root

	trigger = {
		POD_has_any_terrestrial_aspect_trigger = no
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_terrestrial_aspect_air
		add_trait = terrestrial_aspect_air
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_terrestrial_aspect_earth
		add_trait = terrestrial_aspect_earth
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_terrestrial_aspect_fire
		add_trait = terrestrial_aspect_fire
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_terrestrial_aspect_water
		add_trait = terrestrial_aspect_water
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_terrestrial_aspect_wood
		add_trait = terrestrial_aspect_wood
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.4013
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.4013
	}
}

POD_chargen.4013 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.4013.t
	desc = POD_chargen.4013.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root
	
	option = {
		name = immaculates
		set_character_faith = faith:immaculates
	}
	
	option = {
		name = the_dragon_cult
		set_character_faith = faith:the_dragon_cult
	}
	
	option = {
		name = the_enduring
		set_character_faith = faith:the_enduring
	}
	
	option = {
		name = realmists
		set_character_faith = faith:realmists
	}

	after = {
		POD_change_government_if_landed = { GOVERNMENT = immaculate_government }
		hidden_effect = { POD_terrestrial_exalted_initialization_effect = yes } # So all correct effects are run
	}
}

POD_chargen.6000 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.6000.t
	desc = POD_chargen.6000.desc

	theme = faith
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_event_window_widget_chargen_faith_selection"
		container = "foreground_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.6000.a
	}
	
	after = {
		set_character_faith = var:chargen_faith_selection
		
		clear_variable_list = chargen_faiths
		remove_variable = chargen_faith_selection
		remove_variable = chargen_religion
		
		trigger_event = { saved_event_id = scope:next_event }
	}
}
